{"parentUuid":"60fa7da6-20f0-4464-890e-10378a19937d","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"a9b59bca-0aa6-496b-bd57-ae8a961e5155","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018YNwizCDDHwmFzQ4ukJZ3B","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture Rules — StockForge\n2\t\n3\t> Immutable constitution file. Changes require explicit human approval with rationale.\n4\t\n5\t## Clean Architecture Layers\n6\t\n7\t### Layer Hierarchy (mandatory)\n8\t\n9\t```\n10\tinterfaces/     (HTTP, CLI, dashboard, alert handlers)\n11\t    ↓\n12\tapplication/    (use cases, orchestration, ports)\n13\t    ↓\n14\tdomain/         (pure business logic, zero dependencies)\n15\t    ↓\n16\tinfrastructure/ (adapters: Postgres, Redis, R2, vnstock, FiinPro, scrapers, LLMs)\n17\t                — implements application ports\n18\t```\n19\t\n20\t### Import Rules (non-negotiable)\n21\t\n22\t- `domain` imports: **nothing** from other layers. Zero framework. Zero IO. Zero Pydantic. Use stdlib `dataclasses` and `enum`.\n23\t- `application` imports: only from `domain`. Defines ports (Protocol classes).\n24\t- `infrastructure` imports: implements `application` ports. Can import `domain` for types.\n25\t- `interfaces` imports: `application` use cases. Never reaches into `infrastructure` or `domain` directly.\n26\t\n27\t### Why This Matters in Stock Domain\n28\t\n29\t- Pure domain enables **deterministic** valuation logic, testable without DB\n30\t- Swap data providers (vnstock → FiinPro → SSI) without touching domain\n31\t- Backtest the same domain logic on any data source\n32\t- LLM adapters in infrastructure stay isolated from analysis logic\n33\t\n34\t---\n35\t\n36\t## Bounded Contexts (9 BCs)\n37\t\n38\tCross-context communication via shared contracts only.\n39\t\n40\t### BC-1: Market Data\n41\t**Responsibility**: Time-series price/volume, foreign flow, intraday data, market microstructure.\n42\t**Path**: `packages/domain/market_data/`, `apps/*/market_data/`\n43\t**Aggregates**: Quote, Bar, ForeignFlow, OrderBook, MarketRegime\n44\t**Storage**: TimescaleDB hypertables\n45\t\n46\t### BC-2: Fundamental\n47\t**Responsibility**: Financial reports, ratios, valuation inputs.\n48\t**Path**: `packages/domain/fundamental/`\n49\t**Aggregates**: FinancialStatement, Ratio, ValuationInput, EarningsRevision\n50\t**Storage**: Postgres with point-in-time integrity (each report has filing_date + period_end)\n51\t\n52\t### BC-3: Company Intelligence\n53\t**Responsibility**: Company profile, history, leadership, related parties, network relationships.\n54\t**Path**: `packages/domain/company_intelligence/`\n55\t**Aggregates**: Company, Executive, RelatedParty, OwnershipStructure, CorporateAction\n56\t**Storage**: Postgres + graph queries (or Neo4j Phase 3+)\n57\t\n58\t### BC-4: Macro & Policy\n59\t**Responsibility**: Macro indicators (GDP, CPI, lãi suất), regulatory documents, policy changes, geopolitical events.\n60\t**Path**: `packages/domain/macro/`\n61\t**Aggregates**: MacroIndicator, PolicyDocument, RegulatoryEvent, MacroRegime\n62\t**Storage**: TimescaleDB + document store\n63\t\n64\t### BC-5: News Stream\n65\t**Responsibility**: Mainstream financial news ingestion, claim extraction, sentiment, contradiction detection.\n66\t**Path**: `packages/domain/news/`\n67\t**Aggregates**: NewsArticle, ExtractedClaim, NewsCluster, NewsSentimentScore\n68\t**Storage**: Postgres + R2 for full text + pgvector for semantic search\n69\t\n70\t### BC-6: Influence Network ⭐ (the edge)\n71\t**Responsibility**: KOL channel monitoring, recommendation extraction, credibility scoring, calibration.\n72\t**Path**: `packages/domain/influence/`\n73\t**Aggregates**: Kol, Channel, Recommendation, CredibilityScore, InfluenceCluster\n74\t**Storage**: Postgres + R2 for transcripts + calibration database\n75\t\n76\t### BC-7: Crowd Sentiment ⭐ (the edge)\n77\t**Responsibility**: Forum/group/comment sentiment, pump pattern detection, narrative lifecycle tracking.\n78\t**Path**: `packages/domain/crowd/`\n79\t**Aggregates**: SentimentSnapshot, PumpDetection, Narrative, NarrativePhase, CoordinationCluster\n80\t**Storage**: Postgres + TimescaleDB for sentiment time-series\n81\t\n82\t### BC-8: Analysis & Thesis\n83\t**Responsibility**: Multi-perspective adversarial analysis, thesis lifecycle, post-mortem.\n84\t**Path**: `packages/domain/analysis/`\n85\t**Aggregates**: Thesis, BearAnalysis, BullAnalysis, Synthesis, PostMortem, Catalyst\n86\t**Storage**: Postgres + Markdown files in `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/`\n87\t\n88\t### BC-9: Portfolio & Action\n89\t**Responsibility**: Watchlist, alerts, position sizing, risk management, personal bias tracking.\n90\t**Path**: `packages/domain/portfolio/`\n91\t**Aggregates**: Watchlist, Position, RiskRule, Alert, PersonalBias, Decision\n92\t**Storage**: Postgres + append-only Decision log\n93\t\n94\t### Cross-BC Rules\n95\t\n96\t- **Never direct import** between BCs. Cross-BC types live in `packages/contracts/`.\n97\t- **Cross-BC events** defined in `packages/contracts/events/`\n98\t- **Cross-BC commands** defined in `packages/contracts/commands/`\n99\t- Common cross-BC events: `NewsArticleIngested`, `KolRecommendationExtracted`, `PumpDetected`, `ThesisCreated`, `AlertTriggered`\n100\t\n101\t---\n102\t\n103\t## Stack Locked (Phase 1-3)\n104\t\n105\t### Primary: Python\n106\t\n107\t- **Python 3.11+** (strict typing with mypy --strict)\n108\t- **dataclasses** for domain entities (no Pydantic in domain)\n109\t- **Pydantic v2** in interfaces/infrastructure layers only\n110\t- **FastAPI** for HTTP gateway (Phase 2+)\n111\t- **Dramatiq** or **Prefect** for background workers\n112\t- **Click** for CLI tooling\n113\t\n114\t### Data Stack\n115\t\n116\t- **Postgres 16** with extensions:\n117\t  - **TimescaleDB** (time-series for prices, sentiment over time)\n118\t  - **pgvector** (embeddings for semantic search)\n119\t  - **pg_trgm** (Vietnamese fuzzy text search)\n120\t- **Redis** (cache + pub/sub + queue broker)\n121\t- **Cloudflare R2** (S3-compatible, raw content storage)\n122\t- **DuckDB** (ad-hoc analytics, backtesting fast scans)\n123\t\n124\t### LLM\n125\t\n126\t- **Primary**: Claude API\n127\t  - Sonnet 4.6+ for routine extraction, classification\n128\t  - Opus 4.7+ for thesis synthesis, multi-perspective adversarial\n129\t- **Embeddings**: OpenAI text-embedding-3-small (works for Vietnamese)\n130\t- **Whisper**: for YouTube/podcast transcription (local or OpenAI API)\n131\t- **OSS fallback (Phase 4+)**: Llama 3.3 70B via Ollama for cost reduction\n132\t\n133\t### Frontend\n134\t\n135\t- **Phase 1-2**: Streamlit (rapid personal dashboard)\n136\t- **Phase 3+**: Next.js 14 with shadcn/ui (only if SaaS path activated)\n137\t- Always include: TradingView lightweight charts integration\n138\t\n139\t### Workers\n140\t\n141\t- **Python workers**: Dramatiq (async tasks), Prefect (scheduled DAGs)\n142\t- **Schedule examples**:\n143\t  - Hourly: news ingestion, sentiment refresh\n144\t  - Daily 16:00: market data EOD update, screener run\n145\t  - Daily 18:00: KOL channel scan\n146\t  - Weekly Sun: pattern recalibration\n147\t  - Monthly: thesis post-mortem batch\n148\t\n149\t### Infrastructure\n150\t\n151\t- **Monorepo**: pnpm workspaces (even Python apps benefit from it for shared tooling), or Hatch + uv if going pure Python\n152\t- **Deploy (early)**: Single VPS (Hetzner) — personal use\n153\t- **Deploy (scale, if needed)**: Modal/Fly.io for workers, Hetzner dedicated for DB\n154\t- **Observability**: Sentry + simple Grafana for metrics\n155\t\n156\t---\n157\t\n158\t## Folder Conventions\n159\t\n160\t### Per Bounded Context\n161\t\n162\t```\n163\tpackages/domain/<bc_name>/\n164\t├── models/           # Entity classes (rich, with behavior)\n165\t├── value_objects/    # Immutable values (Money, Ticker, Period, etc.)\n166\t├── events/           # Domain events\n167\t├── services/         # Domain services (stateless logic)\n168\t├── repositories/     # Repository protocols (Protocol classes)\n169\t└── __init__.py       # Public barrel export\n170\t\n171\tpackages/application/<bc_name>/\n172\t├── use_cases/        # One class per use case\n173\t├── ports/            # Protocols to infrastructure\n174\t├── commands/         # Command handlers\n175\t├── queries/          # Query handlers\n176\t└── __init__.py\n177\t\n178\tpackages/infrastructure/<bc_name>/\n179\t├── persistence/      # Postgres adapters\n180\t├── external/         # vnstock, FiinPro, scrapers\n181\t├── messaging/        # Event publishers\n182\t└── __init__.py\n183\t\n184\tapps/api/<bc_name>/\n185\t├── routers/          # FastAPI routers\n186\t├── dto/              # Request/response DTOs (Pydantic)\n187\t└── deps.py           # Dependency injection\n188\t```\n189\t\n190\t### File Naming (Python conventions)\n191\t\n192\t- Entities: `company.py`, `thesis.py`, `kol.py`\n193\t- Value objects: `ticker.py`, `money.py`, `period.py`, `as_of_date.py`\n194\t- Events: `kol_recommendation_extracted.py`, `pump_detected.py`\n195\t- Use cases: `validate_thesis_use_case.py`, `extract_kol_recommendation_use_case.py`\n196\t- Repositories: `kol_repository.py` (Protocol), `kol_postgres_repository.py` (impl)\n197\t- Routers: `kols_router.py`\n198\t\n199\t---\n200\t\n201\t## Domain Model Rules\n202\t\n203\t### Rich, Not Anemic\n204\t\n205\tDomain models must have behavior. Not just dataclasses with field access.\n206\t\n207\t**Anti-pattern (anemic)**:\n208\t```python\n209\t@dataclass\n210\tclass Thesis:\n211\t    id: str\n212\t    ticker: str\n213\t    raw_text: str\n214\t    status: str\n215\t# Logic lives in service... wrong\n216\t```\n217\t\n218\t**Correct (rich)**:\n219\t```python\n220\t@dataclass\n221\tclass Thesis:\n222\t    id: ThesisId\n223\t    ticker: Ticker\n224\t    bull_case: BullCase\n225\t    bear_case: BearCase\n226\t    catalysts: list[Catalyst]\n227\t    confidence: ConfidenceScore\n228\t    status: ThesisStatus = ThesisStatus.DRAFT\n229\t    _events: list[DomainEvent] = field(default_factory=list)\n230\t\n231\t    def submit(self) -> None:\n232\t        if self.status != ThesisStatus.DRAFT:\n233\t            raise InvalidStateError(\"Can only submit draft theses\")\n234\t        if not self.bear_case.is_substantive():\n235\t            raise InvariantViolation(\"Thesis must include substantive bear case (charter principle)\")\n236\t        self.status = ThesisStatus.ACTIVE\n237\t        self._events.append(ThesisSubmittedEvent(self.id, self.ticker, datetime.utcnow()))\n238\t```\n239\t\n240\t### Invariants Enforced in Constructors / `__post_init__`\n241\t\n242\tEvery domain object validates its invariants at construction. Invalid states are unrepresentable.\n243\t\n244\t**Stock-specific examples**:\n245\t- `Money` cannot be constructed with negative VND (positive only; for losses use signed wrappers)\n246\t- `Ratio` like P/E ≥ 0; if EPS < 0 → store as None with reason\n247\t- `Recommendation` must have ticker + direction + timeframe + extracted_at + source_url\n248\t- `PumpDetection` must specify phase (incubation/emerging/distribution/dump) with confidence\n249\t\n250\t---\n251\t\n252\t## Event-Driven Rules\n253\t\n254\t### Domain Events\n255\t\n256\t- Every state change emits a domain event\n257\t- Events are past-tense facts (`KolRecommendationExtracted`, not `ExtractKolRecommendation`)\n258\t- Events belong to the aggregate that created them\n259\t- Cross-BC events use shared contract types\n260\t\n261\t### Event Flow\n262\t\n263\t**Phase 1**: Synchronous in-process (simple pub/sub via Redis or in-memory)\n264\t**Phase 2+**: Dramatiq queue for async processing\n265\t**Phase 3+**: Consider event-driven across BCs with proper broker (Redis Streams or NATS)\n266\t\n267\t### Critical Stock-Domain Events\n268\t\n269\t```python\n270\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n271\tclass KolRecommendationExtracted:\n272\t    kol_id: KolId\n273\t    ticker: Ticker\n274\t    direction: Direction        # BUY | HOLD | SELL | WATCH\n275\t    timeframe: Timeframe\n276\t    confidence_extracted: float # how sure LLM is about extraction (NOT recommendation strength)\n277\t    source_url: str\n278\t    source_type: SourceType     # YOUTUBE_VIDEO | FACEBOOK_POST | etc\n279\t    published_at: datetime\n280\t    extracted_at: datetime\n281\t\n282\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n283\tclass PumpDetected:\n284\t    ticker: Ticker\n285\t    phase: PumpPhase\n286\t    phase_confidence: float\n287\t    contributing_signals: list[SignalSnapshot]\n288\t    detected_at: datetime\n289\t\n290\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n291\tclass NarrativePhaseChanged:\n292\t    narrative_id: NarrativeId\n293\t    from_phase: NarrativePhase\n294\t    to_phase: NarrativePhase\n295\t    affected_tickers: list[Ticker]\n296\t    detected_at: datetime\n297\t```\n298\t\n299\t---\n300\t\n301\t## Testing Architecture\n302\t\n303\t### Test Pyramid\n304\t\n305\t```\n306\t           E2E / BDD (slowest, fewest)\n307\t          - Full thesis workflow\n308\t         - Backtest end-to-end\n309\t        Integration\n310\t       - Use case tests with real DB\n311\t      - Adapter tests (vnstock, scrapers)\n312\t     - LLM extractor tests with snapshots\n313\t    Unit (fastest, most)\n314\t   - Pure domain logic\n315\t  - Value objects (Ticker validation, Money math)\n316\t - Domain services (valuation formulas)\n317\t- Pump detection rules\n318\t```\n319\t\n320\t### Stock-Specific Test Categories\n321\t\n322\t- **Domain tests**: pure logic, no LLM, no DB. `pytest packages/domain/`\n323\t- **Backtest tests**: replay historical data, verify decisions. `pytest tests/backtest/`\n324\t- **LLM extractor snapshot tests**: input text → expected structured output. Cached for reproducibility.\n325\t- **Calibration tests**: KOL credibility scores converge correctly given outcome data.\n326\t\n327\t### Location\n328\t\n329\t- Unit tests: co-located `test_*.py` next to source\n330\t- Integration tests: `apps/*/tests/`\n331\t- BDD: `bdd/features/*.feature` + Python step definitions\n332\t- Backtest: `tests/backtest/`\n333\t\n334\t---\n335\t\n336\t## Non-Negotiable Patterns\n337\t\n338\t### 1. Repository Pattern\n339\tAll DB access through repositories defined as Protocols in domain, implemented in infrastructure.\n340\t\n341\t### 2. Use Case Pattern\n342\tApplication layer is use cases, one per business operation. No \"service\" god-classes.\n343\t\n344\t### 3. DTO at Boundary\n345\tHTTP layer uses Pydantic DTOs. Never expose domain entities directly in API responses.\n346\t\n347\t### 4. Mapper Layer\n348\tBetween DTO ↔ Domain ↔ Persistence. Keep boundaries clean.\n349\t\n350\t### 5. CQRS (light)\n351\tCommands (writes) and queries (reads) use different paths. Not full event sourcing — just separation.\n352\t\n353\t### 6. ⭐ No-LLM-Math Pattern\n354\tLLM never returns numeric output as content. LLM returns either:\n355\t- Structured tool call to a deterministic Python function that does the math\n356\t- Classification labels (categorical, not numerical)\n357\t- Free text reasoning (qualitative only)\n358\t\n359\tIf LLM output contains a number, that number must trace to a tool call result.\n360\t\n361\t### 7. ⭐ Point-in-Time Integrity Pattern\n362\tEvery fundamental data row has `filing_date` (when it became known) AND `period_end` (what period it represents). Backtest queries must filter `WHERE filing_date <= as_of_date` to avoid look-ahead bias.\n363\t\n364\t### 8. ⭐ Source Citation Pattern\n365\tEvery claim, every extracted recommendation, every sentiment score has `source_url` + `extracted_at` + `extractor_version`. Ungrounded statements = hallucination = bug.\n366\t\n367\t---\n368\t\n369\t## Forbidden Patterns\n370\t\n371\t- **God services** — single class with 20+ methods doing everything\n372\t- **Anemic domain models** — data bags with logic in services\n373\t- **Direct cross-BC imports** — must go via contracts\n374\t- **Raw SQL in domain** — use repository abstraction\n375\t- **Framework leaks into domain** — no FastAPI, no Pydantic in `packages/domain`\n376\t- **`Any` types** in domain code (mypy --strict enforces)\n377\t- **LLM returning numbers** — must come from code with traceable inputs\n378\t- **Hardcoded position sizes** — must come from PortfolioConfig + RiskRules\n379\t- **Silent ignoring of stale data** — staleness must propagate to UI/output\n380\t- **Single-perspective thesis** — must include bear case explicitly\n381\t\n382\t---\n383\t\n384\t## Evolution Protocol\n385\t\n386\tArchitecture can evolve. When it does:\n387\t1. Document the why in `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`\n388\t2. Update this file with version bump\n389\t3. Run `/drift-check` across codebase to assess impact\n390\t4. Create migration plan if breaking\n391\t\n392\tDo NOT silently diverge from these rules. Either follow them, or change them explicitly.\n393\t\n394\tLast modified: 2026-04-23 (v1.0 initial — adapted from IdeaForge for stock domain)\n395\t"}]},"uuid":"2ac79503-94db-4f42-a4cd-066eac7a359d","timestamp":"2026-04-29T22:16:46.471Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\constitution\\architecture.md","content":"# Architecture Rules — StockForge\n\n> Immutable constitution file. Changes require explicit human approval with rationale.\n\n## Clean Architecture Layers\n\n### Layer Hierarchy (mandatory)\n\n```\ninterfaces/     (HTTP, CLI, dashboard, alert handlers)\n    ↓\napplication/    (use cases, orchestration, ports)\n    ↓\ndomain/         (pure business logic, zero dependencies)\n    ↓\ninfrastructure/ (adapters: Postgres, Redis, R2, vnstock, FiinPro, scrapers, LLMs)\n                — implements application ports\n```\n\n### Import Rules (non-negotiable)\n\n- `domain` imports: **nothing** from other layers. Zero framework. Zero IO. Zero Pydantic. Use stdlib `dataclasses` and `enum`.\n- `application` imports: only from `domain`. Defines ports (Protocol classes).\n- `infrastructure` imports: implements `application` ports. Can import `domain` for types.\n- `interfaces` imports: `application` use cases. Never reaches into `infrastructure` or `domain` directly.\n\n### Why This Matters in Stock Domain\n\n- Pure domain enables **deterministic** valuation logic, testable without DB\n- Swap data providers (vnstock → FiinPro → SSI) without touching domain\n- Backtest the same domain logic on any data source\n- LLM adapters in infrastructure stay isolated from analysis logic\n\n---\n\n## Bounded Contexts (9 BCs)\n\nCross-context communication via shared contracts only.\n\n### BC-1: Market Data\n**Responsibility**: Time-series price/volume, foreign flow, intraday data, market microstructure.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/market_data/`, `apps/*/market_data/`\n**Aggregates**: Quote, Bar, ForeignFlow, OrderBook, MarketRegime\n**Storage**: TimescaleDB hypertables\n\n### BC-2: Fundamental\n**Responsibility**: Financial reports, ratios, valuation inputs.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/fundamental/`\n**Aggregates**: FinancialStatement, Ratio, ValuationInput, EarningsRevision\n**Storage**: Postgres with point-in-time integrity (each report has filing_date + period_end)\n\n### BC-3: Company Intelligence\n**Responsibility**: Company profile, history, leadership, related parties, network relationships.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/company_intelligence/`\n**Aggregates**: Company, Executive, RelatedParty, OwnershipStructure, CorporateAction\n**Storage**: Postgres + graph queries (or Neo4j Phase 3+)\n\n### BC-4: Macro & Policy\n**Responsibility**: Macro indicators (GDP, CPI, lãi suất), regulatory documents, policy changes, geopolitical events.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/macro/`\n**Aggregates**: MacroIndicator, PolicyDocument, RegulatoryEvent, MacroRegime\n**Storage**: TimescaleDB + document store\n\n### BC-5: News Stream\n**Responsibility**: Mainstream financial news ingestion, claim extraction, sentiment, contradiction detection.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/news/`\n**Aggregates**: NewsArticle, ExtractedClaim, NewsCluster, NewsSentimentScore\n**Storage**: Postgres + R2 for full text + pgvector for semantic search\n\n### BC-6: Influence Network ⭐ (the edge)\n**Responsibility**: KOL channel monitoring, recommendation extraction, credibility scoring, calibration.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/influence/`\n**Aggregates**: Kol, Channel, Recommendation, CredibilityScore, InfluenceCluster\n**Storage**: Postgres + R2 for transcripts + calibration database\n\n### BC-7: Crowd Sentiment ⭐ (the edge)\n**Responsibility**: Forum/group/comment sentiment, pump pattern detection, narrative lifecycle tracking.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/crowd/`\n**Aggregates**: SentimentSnapshot, PumpDetection, Narrative, NarrativePhase, CoordinationCluster\n**Storage**: Postgres + TimescaleDB for sentiment time-series\n\n### BC-8: Analysis & Thesis\n**Responsibility**: Multi-perspective adversarial analysis, thesis lifecycle, post-mortem.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/analysis/`\n**Aggregates**: Thesis, BearAnalysis, BullAnalysis, Synthesis, PostMortem, Catalyst\n**Storage**: Postgres + Markdown files in `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/`\n\n### BC-9: Portfolio & Action\n**Responsibility**: Watchlist, alerts, position sizing, risk management, personal bias tracking.\n**Path**: `packages/domain/portfolio/`\n**Aggregates**: Watchlist, Position, RiskRule, Alert, PersonalBias, Decision\n**Storage**: Postgres + append-only Decision log\n\n### Cross-BC Rules\n\n- **Never direct import** between BCs. Cross-BC types live in `packages/contracts/`.\n- **Cross-BC events** defined in `packages/contracts/events/`\n- **Cross-BC commands** defined in `packages/contracts/commands/`\n- Common cross-BC events: `NewsArticleIngested`, `KolRecommendationExtracted`, `PumpDetected`, `ThesisCreated`, `AlertTriggered`\n\n---\n\n## Stack Locked (Phase 1-3)\n\n### Primary: Python\n\n- **Python 3.11+** (strict typing with mypy --strict)\n- **dataclasses** for domain entities (no Pydantic in domain)\n- **Pydantic v2** in interfaces/infrastructure layers only\n- **FastAPI** for HTTP gateway (Phase 2+)\n- **Dramatiq** or **Prefect** for background workers\n- **Click** for CLI tooling\n\n### Data Stack\n\n- **Postgres 16** with extensions:\n  - **TimescaleDB** (time-series for prices, sentiment over time)\n  - **pgvector** (embeddings for semantic search)\n  - **pg_trgm** (Vietnamese fuzzy text search)\n- **Redis** (cache + pub/sub + queue broker)\n- **Cloudflare R2** (S3-compatible, raw content storage)\n- **DuckDB** (ad-hoc analytics, backtesting fast scans)\n\n### LLM\n\n- **Primary**: Claude API\n  - Sonnet 4.6+ for routine extraction, classification\n  - Opus 4.7+ for thesis synthesis, multi-perspective adversarial\n- **Embeddings**: OpenAI text-embedding-3-small (works for Vietnamese)\n- **Whisper**: for YouTube/podcast transcription (local or OpenAI API)\n- **OSS fallback (Phase 4+)**: Llama 3.3 70B via Ollama for cost reduction\n\n### Frontend\n\n- **Phase 1-2**: Streamlit (rapid personal dashboard)\n- **Phase 3+**: Next.js 14 with shadcn/ui (only if SaaS path activated)\n- Always include: TradingView lightweight charts integration\n\n### Workers\n\n- **Python workers**: Dramatiq (async tasks), Prefect (scheduled DAGs)\n- **Schedule examples**:\n  - post-mortem batch\n\n### Infrastructure\n\n- **Monorepo**: pnpm workspaces (even Python apps benefit from it for shared tooling), or Hatch + uv if going pure Python\n- **Deploy (early)**: Single VPS (Hetzner) — personal use\n- **Deploy (scale, if needed)**: Modal/Fly.io for workers, Hetzner dedicated for DB\n- **Observability**: Sentry + simple Grafana for metrics\n\n---\n\n## Folder Conventions\n\n### Per Bounded Context\n\n```\npackages/domain/<bc_name>/\n├── models/           # Entity classes (rich, with behavior)\n├── value_objects/    # Immutable values (Money, Ticker, Period, etc.)\n├── events/           # Domain events\n├── services/         # Domain services (stateless logic)\n├── repositories/     # Repository protocols (Protocol classes)\n└── __init__.py       # Public barrel export\n\npackages/application/<bc_name>/\n├── use_cases/        # One class per use case\n├── ports/            # Protocols to infrastructure\n├── commands/         # Command handlers\n├── queries/          # Query handlers\n└── __init__.py\n\npackages/infrastructure/<bc_name>/\n├── persistence/      # Postgres adapters\n├── external/         # vnstock, FiinPro, `as_of_date.py`\n- Events: `kol_recommendation_extracted.py`, `pump_detected.py`\n- Use cases: `validate_thesis_use_case.py`, `extract_kol_recommendation_use_case.py`\n- Repositories: `kol_repository.py` (Protocol), `kol_postgres_repository.py` (impl)\n- Routers: `kols_router.py`\n\n---\n\n## Domain Model Rules\n\n### Rich, Not Anemic\n\nDomain models must have behavior. Not just dataclasses with field access.\n\n**Anti-pattern (anemic)**:\n```python\n@dataclass\nclass Thesis:\n    id: str\n    ticker: str\n    raw_text: str\n    status: str\n# Logic lives in service... wrong\n```\n\n**Correct (rich)**:\n```python\n@dataclass\nclass Thesis:\n    id: ThesisId\n    ticker: Ticker\n    bull_case: BullCase\n    bear_case: BearCase\n    catalysts: list[Catalyst]\n    confidence: ConfidenceScore\n    status: ThesisStatus = ThesisStatus.DRAFT\n    _events: list[DomainEvent] = field(default_factory=list)\n\n    def submit(self) -> None:\n        if self.status != ThesisStatus.DRAFT:\n            raise InvalidStateError(\"Can only submit draft theses\")\n        if not self.bear_case.is_substantive():\n            raise InvariantViolation(\"Thesis must include substantive bear case (charter principle)\")\n        self.status = ThesisStatus.ACTIVE\n        self._events.append(ThesisSubmittedEvent(self.id, self.ticker, datetime.utcnow()))\n```\n\n### Invariants Enforced in Constructors / `__post_init__`\n\nEvery domain object validates its invariants at construction. Invalid states are unrepresentable.\n\n**Stock-specific examples**:\n- `Money` cannot be constructed with negative VND (positive only; for losses use signed wrappers)\n- `Ratio` like P/E ≥ 0; if EPS < 0 → store as None with reason\n- `Recommendation` must have ticker + direction + timeframe + extracted_at + source_url\n- `PumpDetection` must specify phase (incubation/emerging/distribution/dump) with confidence\n\n---\n\n## Event-Driven Rules\n\n### Domain Events\n\n- Every state change emits a domain event\n- Events are past-tense facts (`KolRecommendationExtracted`, not `ExtractKolRecommendation`)\n- Events belong to the aggregate that created them\n- Cross-BC events use shared contract types\n\n### Event Flow\n\n**Phase 1**: Synchronous in-process (simple pub/sub via Redis or in-memory)\n**Phase 2+**: Dramatiq queue for async processing\n**Phase 3+**: Consider event-driven across BCs with proper broker (Redis Streams or NATS)\n\n### Critical Stock-Domain Events\n\n```python\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass KolRecommendationExtracted:\n    kol_id: KolId\n    ticker: Ticker\n    direction: Direction        # BUY | HOLD | SELL | WATCH\n    timeframe: Timeframe\n    confidence_extracted: float # how sure LLM is about extraction (NOT recommendation strength)\n    source_url: str\n    source_type: SourceType     # YOUTUBE_VIDEO | FACEBOOK_POST | etc\n    published_at: datetime\n    extracted_at: datetime\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass PumpDetected:\n    ticker: Ticker\n    phase: PumpPhase\n    phase_confidence: float\n    contributing_signals: list[SignalSnapshot]\n    detected_at: datetime\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass NarrativePhaseChanged:\n    narrative_id: NarrativeId\n    from_phase: NarrativePhase\n    to_phase: NarrativePhase\n    affected_tickers: list[Ticker]\n    detected_at: datetime\n```\n\n---\n\n## Testing Architecture\n\n### Test Pyramid\n\n```\n           E2E / BDD (slowest, fewest)\n          - Full thesis workflow\n         - Backtest end-to-end\n        Integration\n       - Use case tests with real DB\n      - Adapter tests (vnstock, scrapers)\n     - LLM extractor tests with snapshots\n    Unit (fastest, most)\n   - Pure domain logic\n  - Value objects (Ticker validation, Money math)\n - Domain services (valuation formulas)\n- Pump detection rules\n```\n\n### Stock-Specific Test Categories\n\n- **Domain tests**: pure logic, no LLM, no DB. `pytest packages/domain/`\n- **Backtest tests**: replay historical data, verify decisions. `pytest tests/backtest/`\n- **LLM extractor snapshot tests**: input text → expected structured output. Cached for reproducibility.\n- **Calibration tests**: KOL credibility scores converge correctly given outcome data.\n\n### Location\n\n- Unit tests: co-located `test_*.py` next to source\n- Integration tests: `apps/*/tests/`\n- BDD: `bdd/features/*.feature` + Python step definitions\n- Backtest: `tests/backtest/`\n\n---\n\n## Non-Negotiable Patterns\n\n### 1. Repository Pattern\nAll DB access through repositories defined as Protocols in domain, implemented in infrastructure.\n\n### 2. Use Case Pattern\nApplication layer is use cases, one per business operation. No \"service\" god-classes.\n\n### 3. DTO at Boundary\nHTTP layer uses Pydantic DTOs. Never expose domain entities directly in API responses.\n\n### 4. Mapper Layer\nBetween DTO ↔ Domain ↔ Persistence. Keep boundaries clean.\n\n### 5. CQRS (light)\nCommands (writes) and queries (reads) use different paths. Not full event sourcing — just separation.\n\n### 6. ⭐ No-LLM-Math Pattern\nLLM never returns numeric output as content. LLM returns either:\n- Structured tool call to a deterministic Python function that does the math\n- Classification labels (categorical, not numerical)\n- Free text reasoning (qualitative only)\n\nIf LLM output contains a number, that number must trace to a tool call result.\n\n### 7. ⭐ Point-in-Time Integrity Pattern\nEvery fundamental data row has `filing_date` (when it became known) AND `period_end` (what period it represents). Backtest queries must filter `WHERE filing_date <= as_of_date` to avoid look-ahead bias.\n\n### 8. ⭐ Source Citation Pattern\nEvery claim, every extracted recommendation, every sentiment score has `source_url` + `extracted_at` + `extractor_version`. Ungrounded statements = hallucination = bug.\n\n---\n\n## Forbidden Patterns\n\n- **God services** — single class with 20+ methods doing everything\n- **Anemic domain models** — data bags with logic in services\n- **Direct cross-BC imports** — must go via contracts\n- **Raw SQL in domain** — use repository abstraction\n- **Framework leaks into domain** — no FastAPI, no Pydantic in `packages/domain`\n- **`Any` types** in domain code (mypy --strict enforces)\n- **LLM returning numbers** — must come from code with traceable inputs\n- **Hardcoded position sizes** — must come from PortfolioConfig + RiskRules\n- **Silent ignoring of stale data** — staleness must propagate to UI/output\n- **Single-perspective thesis** — must include bear case explicitly\n\n---\n\n## Evolution Protocol\n\nArchitecture can evolve. When it does:\n1. Document the why in `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`\n2. Update this file with version bump\n3. Run `/drift-check` across codebase to assess impact\n4. Create migration plan if breaking\n\nDo NOT silently diverge from these rules. Either follow them, or change them explicitly.\n\nLast modified: 2026-04-23 (v1.0 initial — adapted from IdeaForge for stock domain)\n","numLines":395,"startLine":1,"totalLines":395}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"60fa7da6-20f0-4464-890e-10378a19937d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d4111a7-6f57-4079-8f2d-7d1e44eadd1a","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"d0eff2ac-7d3e-48b6-9154-791a08fd39b4","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"a9b59bca-0aa6-496b-bd57-ae8a961e5155","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Cg7h39BoW6kwrCh1Uqt2zK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# sync-tracker-update.sh — Track 8a Confidence Score System update hook\n3\t# Per D-006 (IMPL-S17-1): bash+awk only; no python/jq/yq/sqlite3 (L-S11-1 portability).\n4\t#\n5\t# Usage:\n6\t#   sync-tracker-update.sh <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\n7\t#\n8\t# If <override_delta> is empty/omitted, the hook looks up `weight_<event_type>` from weights.yaml.\n9\t# This preserves the tunable-weights contract: edit weights.yaml once, all callers pick up new values.\n10\t# Override mode is for testing or non-standard partial-credit events.\n11\t#\n12\t# Categories: LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | SCOPE | DECISION_ROUTING\n13\t# Event types: decision_correctness | charter_match | q_and_a_resolution\n14\t#              drift_signal | decision_correction | decision_revocation | thesis_revocation\n15\t#\n16\t# Behavior:\n17\t#   1. Resolve delta from weights.yaml if not overridden.\n18\t#   2. Append event row to events.tsv (atomic append).\n19\t#   3. Recompute state.tsv from full events.tsv replay.\n20\t#   4. Decrement must_grill_remaining for the affected category if >0.\n21\t#   5. Call sync-tracker-render.sh to regenerate _index.md (best-effort; log if fails).\n22\t\n23\tset -euo pipefail\n24\t\n25\tROOT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)\"\n26\tSYNC_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker\"\n27\tEVENTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\"\n28\tSTATE=\"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\"\n29\tWEIGHTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/weights.yaml\"\n30\tRENDER_HOOK=\"$ROOT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\n31\tLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n32\t\n33\tCAT=\"${1:-}\"\n34\tEVT=\"${2:-}\"\n35\tOVERRIDE_DELTA=\"${3:-}\"\n36\tDEC_ID=\"${4:-}\"\n37\tSOURCE_EV=\"${5:-}\"\n38\tREASON=\"${6:-}\"\n39\t\n40\tif [[ -z \"$CAT\" || -z \"$EVT\" ]]; then\n41\t  echo \"[sync-tracker-update] usage: $0 <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\" >&2\n42\t  exit 2\n43\tfi\n44\t\n45\t# Validate category\n46\tcase \"$CAT\" in\n47\t  LANGUAGE|DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS|DESIGN_THINKING|SCOPE|DECISION_ROUTING) ;;\n48\t  *) echo \"[sync-tracker-update] invalid category: $CAT\" >&2; exit 2 ;;\n49\tesac\n50\t\n51\t# Resolve delta: override CLI arg wins; else look up weight_<event_type> from weights.yaml.\n52\tget_weight() { awk -F': *' -v key=\"$1\" '$1==key {print $2; exit}' \"$WEIGHTS\"; }\n53\tif [[ -n \"$OVERRIDE_DELTA\" ]]; then\n54\t  DELTA=\"$OVERRIDE_DELTA\"\n55\telse\n56\t  DELTA=\"$(get_weight \"weight_$EVT\")\"\n57\t  if [[ -z \"$DELTA\" ]]; then\n58\t    echo \"[sync-tracker-update] no weight defined for event_type=$EVT; pass override_delta or add weight_$EVT to weights.yaml\" >&2\n59\t    exit 2\n60\t  fi\n61\tfi\n62\t\n63\tTS=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"\n64\t\n65\t# Append event (TSV; tab-separated; replace tabs in fields with spaces to keep single line)\n66\tsanitize() { printf '%s' \"${1:-}\" | tr '\\t\\n' '  '; }\n67\tprintf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n68\t  \"$TS\" \"$CAT\" \"$EVT\" \"$DELTA\" \\\n69\t  \"$(sanitize \"$DEC_ID\")\" \"$(sanitize \"$SOURCE_EV\")\" \"$(sanitize \"$REASON\")\" \\\n70\t  >> \"$EVENTS\"\n71\t\n72\t# --- Recompute state.tsv ---\n73\t# Read initial scores from weights.yaml (category_<NAME>: <int>)\n74\tINIT_LANG=\"$(get_weight category_LANGUAGE)\"\n75\tINIT_DOM=\"$(get_weight category_DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS)\"\n76\tINIT_DES=\"$(get_weight category_DESIGN_THINKING)\"\n77\tINIT_SCO=\"$(get_weight category_SCOPE)\"\n78\tINIT_DEC=\"$(get_weight category_DECISION_ROUTING)\"\n79\t\n80\t# Preserve must_grill_remaining from existing state.tsv (so decrement persists across runs)\n81\tget_grill() { awk -F'\\t' -v cat=\"$1\" 'NR>1 && $1==cat {print $5; exit}' \"$STATE\"; }\n82\tGRILL_LANG=\"$(get_grill LANGUAGE)\"; GRILL_LANG=\"${GRILL_LANG:-0}\"\n83\tGRILL_DOM=\"$(get_grill DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS)\"; GRILL_DOM=\"${GRILL_DOM:-0}\"\n84\tGRILL_DES=\"$(get_grill DESIGN_THINKING)\"; GRILL_DES=\"${GRILL_DES:-0}\"\n85\tGRILL_SCO=\"$(get_grill SCOPE)\"; GRILL_SCO=\"${GRILL_SCO:-0}\"\n86\tGRILL_DEC=\"$(get_grill DECISION_ROUTING)\"; GRILL_DEC=\"${GRILL_DEC:-0}\"\n87\t\n88\t# Reversal protocol: revocation events set must_grill_remaining=5\n89\tRVC=\"$(get_weight reversal_must_grill_cycles)\"; RVC=\"${RVC:-5}\"\n90\tcase \"$EVT\" in\n91\t  decision_revocation|thesis_revocation)\n92\t    case \"$CAT\" in\n93\t      LANGUAGE) GRILL_LANG=\"$RVC\" ;;\n94\t      DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS) GRILL_DOM=\"$RVC\" ;;\n95\t      DESIGN_THINKING) GRILL_DES=\"$RVC\" ;;\n96\t      SCOPE) GRILL_SCO=\"$RVC\" ;;\n97\t      DECISION_ROUTING) GRILL_DEC=\"$RVC\" ;;\n98\t    esac\n99\t    ;;\n100\tesac\n101\t\n102\t# Replay events.tsv to compute current scores + sample counts + last_updated_ts.\n103\tawk -F'\\t' -v il=\"$INIT_LANG\" -v id=\"$INIT_DOM\" -v ie=\"$INIT_DES\" -v is=\"$INIT_SCO\" -v ir=\"$INIT_DEC\" \\\n104\t    -v gl=\"$GRILL_LANG\" -v gd=\"$GRILL_DOM\" -v ge=\"$GRILL_DES\" -v gs=\"$GRILL_SCO\" -v gr=\"$GRILL_DEC\" '\n105\tBEGIN {\n106\t  s[\"LANGUAGE\"]=il; s[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=id; s[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=ie; s[\"SCOPE\"]=is; s[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=ir;\n107\t  c[\"LANGUAGE\"]=0; c[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=0; c[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=0; c[\"SCOPE\"]=0; c[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=0;\n108\t  t[\"LANGUAGE\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\"; t[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n109\t  t[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\"; t[\"SCOPE\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n110\t  t[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n111\t  g[\"LANGUAGE\"]=gl; g[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=gd; g[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=ge; g[\"SCOPE\"]=gs; g[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=gr;\n112\t  cats=\"LANGUAGE DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS DESIGN_THINKING SCOPE DECISION_ROUTING\";\n113\t}\n114\tNR==1 { next }  # skip header\n115\t$2 in s {\n116\t  s[$2] += $4 + 0;\n117\t  if (s[$2] < 0) s[$2] = 0;\n118\t  if (s[$2] > 100) s[$2] = 100;\n119\t  c[$2] += 1;\n120\t  if ($1 > t[$2]) t[$2] = $1;\n121\t}\n122\tEND {\n123\t  print \"category\\tcurrent_score\\tsample_count\\tlast_updated_ts\\tmust_grill_remaining\";\n124\t  n=split(cats, arr, \" \");\n125\t  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) {\n126\t    k=arr[i];\n127\t    printf \"%s\\t%g\\t%d\\t%s\\t%d\\n\", k, s[k], c[k], t[k], g[k];\n128\t  }\n129\t}\n130\t' \"$EVENTS\" > \"$STATE.tmp\" && mv \"$STATE.tmp\" \"$STATE\"\n131\t\n132\t# Decrement must_grill_remaining for the just-affected category (if it was >0 before this event)\n133\t# (Decrement happens AFTER state replay so the recorded grill count reflects post-event state.)\n134\tcase \"$EVT\" in\n135\t  decision_revocation|thesis_revocation) ;;  # already set fresh above\n136\t  *)\n137\t    awk -F'\\t' -v cat=\"$CAT\" -v OFS='\\t' '\n138\tNR==1 { print; next }\n139\t$1==cat && $5+0 > 0 { $5 = $5 - 1; print; next }\n140\t{ print }\n141\t' \"$STATE\" > \"$STATE.tmp\" && mv \"$STATE.tmp\" \"$STATE\"\n142\t    ;;\n143\tesac\n144\t\n145\t# Render _index.md (best-effort)\n146\tif [[ -x \"$RENDER_HOOK\" ]]; then\n147\t  \"$RENDER_HOOK\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"[sync-tracker-update] render hook failed (non-blocking)\" >> \"$LOG\"\n148\tfi\n149\t\n150\techo \"[sync-tracker-update] $TS  $CAT  $EVT  delta=$DELTA  decision=$DEC_ID\" >> \"$LOG\"\n151\texit 0\n152\t"}]},"uuid":"44b33c29-31b2-4325-88b6-69649a3f07e8","timestamp":"2026-04-29T22:17:38.281Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\sync-tracker-update.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# sync-tracker-update.sh — Track 8a Confidence Score System update hook\n# Per D-006 (IMPL-S17-1): bash+awk only; Resolve delta from weights.yaml if not overridden.\n#   2. Append event row to events.tsv (atomic append).\n#   3. Recompute state.tsv from full events.tsv replay.\n#   4. Decrement must_grill_remaining for the affected category if >0.\n#   5. Call sync-tracker-render.sh to regenerate _index.md (best-effort; log if fails).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nROOT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)\"\nSYNC_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker\"\nEVENTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\"\nSTATE=\"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\"\nWEIGHTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/weights.yaml\"\nRENDER_HOOK=\"$ROOT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\nLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nCAT=\"${1:-}\"\nEVT=\"${2:-}\"\nOVERRIDE_DELTA=\"${3:-}\"\nDEC_ID=\"${4:-}\"\nSOURCE_EV=\"${5:-}\"\nREASON=\"${6:-}\"\n\nif [[ -z \"$CAT\" || -z \"$EVT\" ]]; then\n  echo \"[sync-tracker-update] usage: $0 <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\n# Validate category\ncase \"$CAT\" in\n  LANGUAGE|DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS|DESIGN_THINKING|SCOPE|DECISION_ROUTING) ;;\n  *) echo \"[sync-tracker-update] invalid category: $CAT\" >&2; exit 2 ;;\nesac\n\n# Resolve delta: override CLI arg wins; else look up weight_<event_type> from weights.yaml.\nget_weight() { awk -F': *' -v key=\"$1\" '$1==key {print $2; exit}' \"$WEIGHTS\"; }\nif [[ -n \"$OVERRIDE_DELTA\" ]]; then\n  DELTA=\"$OVERRIDE_DELTA\"\nelse\n  DELTA=\"$(get_weight \"weight_$EVT\")\"\n  if [[ -z \"$DELTA\" ]]; then\n    echo \"[sync-tracker-update] no weight defined for event_type=$EVT; pass override_delta or add weight_$EVT to weights.yaml\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nTS=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"\n\n# Append event (TSV; tab-separated; replace tabs in fields with spaces to keep single line)\nsanitize() { printf '%s' \"${1:-}\" | tr '\\t\\n' '  '; }\nprintf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$CAT\" \"$EVT\" \"$DELTA\" \\\n  \"$(sanitize \"$DEC_ID\")\" \"$(sanitize \"$SOURCE_EV\")\" \"$(sanitize \"$REASON\")\" \\\n  >> \"$EVENTS\"\n\n# --- Recompute state.tsv ---\n# Read initial scores from weights.yaml (category_<NAME>: <int>)\nINIT_LANG=\"$(get_weight category_LANGUAGE)\"\nINIT_DOM=\"$(get_weight category_DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS)\"\nINIT_DES=\"$(get_weight category_DESIGN_THINKING)\"\nINIT_SCO=\"$(get_weight category_SCOPE)\"\nINIT_DEC=\"$(get_weight category_DECISION_ROUTING)\"\n\n# Preserve must_grill_remaining from existing state.tsv (so decrement persists across runs)\nget_grill() { awk -F'\\t' -v cat=\"$1\" 'NR>1 && $1==cat {print $5; exit}' \"$STATE\"; }\nGRILL_LANG=\"$(get_grill LANGUAGE)\"; GRILL_LANG=\"${GRILL_LANG:-0}\"\nGRILL_DOM=\"$(get_grill DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS)\"; GRILL_DOM=\"${GRILL_DOM:-0}\"\nGRILL_DES=\"$(get_grill DESIGN_THINKING)\"; GRILL_DES=\"${GRILL_DES:-0}\"\nGRILL_SCO=\"$(get_grill SCOPE)\"; GRILL_SCO=\"${GRILL_SCO:-0}\"\nGRILL_DEC=\"$(get_grill DECISION_ROUTING)\"; GRILL_DEC=\"${GRILL_DEC:-0}\"\n\n# Reversal protocol: revocation events set must_grill_remaining=5\nRVC=\"$(get_weight reversal_must_grill_cycles)\"; RVC=\"${RVC:-5}\"\ncase \"$EVT\" in\n  decision_revocation|thesis_revocation)\n    case \"$CAT\" in\n      LANGUAGE) GRILL_LANG=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS) GRILL_DOM=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      DESIGN_THINKING) GRILL_DES=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      SCOPE) GRILL_SCO=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      DECISION_ROUTING) GRILL_DEC=\"$RVC\" ;;\n    esac\n    ;;\nesac\n\n# Replay events.tsv to compute current scores + sample counts + last_updated_ts.\nawk -F'\\t' -v il=\"$INIT_LANG\" -v id=\"$INIT_DOM\" -v ie=\"$INIT_DES\" -v is=\"$INIT_SCO\" -v ir=\"$INIT_DEC\" \\\n    -v gl=\"$GRILL_LANG\" -v gd=\"$GRILL_DOM\" -v ge=\"$GRILL_DES\" -v gs=\"$GRILL_SCO\" -v gr=\"$GRILL_DEC\" '\nBEGIN {\n  s[\"LANGUAGE\"]=il; s[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=id; s[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=ie; s[\"SCOPE\"]=is; s[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=ir;\n  c[\"LANGUAGE\"]=0; c[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=0; c[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=0; c[\"SCOPE\"]=0; c[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=0;\n  t[\"LANGUAGE\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\"; t[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n  t[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\"; t[\"SCOPE\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n  t[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n  g[\"LANGUAGE\"]=gl; g[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=gd; g[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=ge; g[\"SCOPE\"]=gs; g[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=gr;\n  cats=\"LANGUAGE DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS DESIGN_THINKING SCOPE DECISION_ROUTING\";\n}\nNR==1 { next }  # skip header\n$2 in s {\n  s[$2] += $4 + 0;\n  if (s[$2] < 0) s[$2] = 0;\n  if (s[$2] > 100) s[$2] = 100;\n  c[$2] += 1;\n  if ($1 > t[$2]) t[$2] = $1;\n}\nEND {\n  print \"category\\tcurrent_score\\tsample_count\\tlast_updated_ts\\tmust_grill_remaining\";\n  n=split(cats, arr, \" \");\n  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) {\n    k=arr[i];\n    printf \"%s\\t%g\\t%d\\t%s\\t%d\\n\", k, s[k], c[k], t[k], g[k];\n  }\n}\n' \"$EVENTS\" > \"$STATE.tmp\" && mv \"$STATE.tmp\" \"$STATE\"\n\n# Decrement must_grill_remaining for the just-affected category (if it was >0 before this event)\n# (Decrement happens AFTER state replay so the recorded grill count reflects post-event state.)\ncase \"$EVT\" in\n  decision_revocation|thesis_revocation) ;;  # already set fresh above\n  *)\n    awk -F'\\t' -v cat=\"$CAT\" -v OFS='\\t' '\nNR==1 { print; next }\n$1==cat && $5+0 > 0 { $5 = $5 - 1; print; next }\n{ print }\n' \"$STATE\" > \"$STATE.tmp\" && mv \"$STATE.tmp\" \"$STATE\"\n    ;;\nesac\n\n# Render _index.md (best-effort)\nif [[ -x \"$RENDER_HOOK\" ]]; then\n  \"$RENDER_HOOK\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"[sync-tracker-update] render hook failed (non-blocking)\" >> \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\necho \"[sync-tracker-update] $TS  $CAT  $EVT  delta=$DELTA  decision=$DEC_ID\" >> \"$LOG\"\nexit 0\n","numLines":152,"startLine":1,"totalLines":152}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d0eff2ac-7d3e-48b6-9154-791a08fd39b4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d4111a7-6f57-4079-8f2d-7d1e44eadd1a","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"a169070c-1588-4a00-a333-787a4cbb65f3","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01TD3PJyeiKivY9Gsc8fKjdN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NQb1Fn13zSZzEuG4xDJ82u","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\domain\\news\\README.md","content":"# BC-5: News Stream\n\n> Tier 2 (Official Narrative) — mainstream news + broker reports + government disclosures.\n\n**Responsibility**: Mainstream financial news ingestion, claim extraction, sentiment, contradiction detection.\n\n**Aggregates**: NewsArticle, ExtractedClaim, NewsCluster, NewsSentimentScore\n\n**Storage**: Postgres for metadata + extracted claims; R2 for full article text; pgvector for semantic search.\n\n**Sources** (Phase 1):\n- CafeF (cafef.vn) — most popular VN financial news\n- Vietstock News (vietstock.vn) — second most popular\n- NDH (nhipcaudautu.vn) — quality analysis\n- VietnamBiz, Đầu Tư Chứng Khoán\n- Broker reports: SSI, HSC, VCSC, VND, FPTS public summaries\n\n**LLM role**:\n- Extract structured claims from unstructured news\n- Classify sentiment categorically (STRONGLY_BULLISH | BULLISH | NEUTRAL | BEARISH | STRONGLY_BEARISH) — NEVER numeric\n- Identify mentioned entities (tickers, sectors, people)\n- Detect contradictions between sources\n- Cluster related stories\n\n**Update cadence**: news polling every 10 min; {"parentUuid":"fea4b721-a00a-4b06-aedb-a0e2da80b71b","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"a9b59bca-0aa6-496b-bd57-ae8a961e5155","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NQb1Fn13zSZzEuG4xDJ82u","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\domain\\news\\README.md"}]},"uuid":"1eda5361-235d-44b9-bb52-48eb7e3b97ad","timestamp":"2026-04-29T22:19:30.274Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\domain\\news\\README.md","content":"# BC-5: News Stream\n\n> Tier 2 (Official Narrative) — mainstream news + broker reports + government disclosures.\n\n**Responsibility**: Mainstream financial news ingestion, claim extraction, sentiment, contradiction detection.\n\n**Aggregates**: NewsArticle, ExtractedClaim, NewsCluster, NewsSentimentScore\n\n**Storage**: Postgres for metadata + extracted claims; R2 for full article text; pgvector for semantic search.\n\n**Sources** (Phase 1):\n- CafeF (cafef.vn) — most popular VN financial news\n- Vietstock News (vietstock.vn) — second most popular\n- NDH (nhipcaudautu.vn) — quality analysis\n- VietnamBiz, Đầu Tư Chứng Khoán\n- Broker reports: SSI, HSC, VCSC, VND, FPTS public summaries\n\n**LLM role**:\n- Extract structured claims from unstructured news\n- Classify sentiment categorically (STRONGLY_BULLISH | BULLISH | NEUTRAL | BEARISH | STRONGLY_BEARISH) — NEVER numeric\n- Identify mentioned entities (tickers, sectors, people)\n- Detect contradictions between sources\n- Cluster related stories\n\n**Update cadence**: news polling every 10 min; broker reports daily 18:00; regulatory every 4 hours.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fea4b721-a00a-4b06-aedb-a0e2da80b71b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d4111a7-6f57-4079-8f2d-7d1e44eadd1a","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"1ac3cee7-eddb-4856-be11-d06b269689ce","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01TD3PJyeiKivY9Gsc8fKjdN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018M3B5K2b9r2rvrcKFoVaaj","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\domain\\influence\\README.md","content":"# BC-6: Influence Network ⭐ (the edge)\n\n> Tier 3 (Influence Network) — KOL channels + per-KOL credibility calibration. Compounding moat over 12-24 months.\n\n**Responsibility**: KOL channel monitoring, recommendation extraction, credibility scoring, calibration.\n\n**Aggregates**: Kol, Channel, Recommendation, CredibilityScore, InfluenceCluster\n\n**Storage**: Postgres for KOL profiles + recommendations + calibration; R2 for video/podcast transcripts.\n\n**Sources** (Phase 1-2):\n- YouTube channels — yt-dlp + Whisper transcription\n- Facebook fanpages (public, ToS-respecting) — read-only\n- Telegram public channels — read-only bot\n- Podcasts — RSS + Whisper\n- Personal blogs / Substack\n\n**LLM role**:\n- Extract recommendations (timestamp, intent: STRONG_BUY|BUY|WATCH|NEUTRAL|AVOID|STRONG_AVOID, conditions, timeframe)\n- Detect coordinated messaging (same KOL, multiple stocks, pattern)\n- Classify KOL style (FUNDAMENTAL | TECHNICAL | NARRATIVE | PUMPER | MIXED)\n- Identify view-flips (was bullish, now bearish — flip-flop frequency)\n\n**Calibration database**: per-KOL hit rate over time; sector-specific scores; Bayesian confidence interval. After 12 months → statistically meaningful per-KOL credibility.\n\nSee `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` for full implementation.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1286,"cache_read_input_tokens":169079,"output_tokens":6086,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1286,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":6086,"cache_read_input_tokens":169079,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1286,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1286},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaYqesNQK8znmU4W3dkSg","type":"assistant","uuid":"30f00327-1da1-48f6-9c02-b7219022b135","timestamp":"2026-04-29T22:19:40.700Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d4111a7-6f57-4079-8f2d-7d1e44eadd1a","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"d1a2928d-c7fc-4fe8-96ea-30375dd3e4cc","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01TD3PJyeiKivY9Gsc8fKjdN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01KkGdeGKrjr4yAbnksr3Whb","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\domain\\portfolio\\README.md","content":"# BC-9: Portfolio & Action\n\n> Watchlist + alerts + position sizing + risk management.
